========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Yeah, definitely. I can see it in the mirror, and like I've been saying, my jeans are getting really baggy on me. It's just because my eating has been so completely off for the last few weeks. Skipping breakfast and lunch without even realizing it, and then barely touching the dinners my wife makes. 

She bought me these jeans, actually. From one of those outlet malls out past the city. We drove out there on a Saturday, which was a terrible idea. The traffic was unbelievable. But yeah, the jeans she got me that day, they're definitely fitting a lot looser now. So I definitely look thinner than I did a few weeks ago.

It's frustrating because I just bought this pair, and finding jeans that fit right is a total nightmare now. They always change the cuts, you know? You find a brand you like and then boom, they discontinue it. But, uh... yeah, so the food and the clothes not fitting thing is mostly why I'm here.\n\nDoctor: Have you been trying to lose weight?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I mean, I've never really been a gym guy or someone who counts calories. I tried counting calories once back in my twenties, but it was just so tedious entering everything into that little app on my phone. And honestly, I'm not great with phones anyway. I just had to upgrade mine because the battery kept dying at like 40 percent, and the guy at the store tried to sell me all these accessories I didn't need. They always do that, right? They see you coming and try to tack on a case and a screen protector and a car charger. I just wanted a phone that actually turns on when I need it to. But no, I haven't been trying to lose weight or anything like that. It's just happening.\n\nDoctor: Has something stressful or upsetting happened to you recently?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, nothing stands out. Life's just been pretty normal lately. Well, as normal as it can be when you're just hanging around the house all day. My wife has been working a lot, she's a dental hygienist, and they've been short-staffed because one of the other hygienists went on maternity leave. Actually, she had twins, which is wild. I can't even imagine having two babies at the exact same time. The stroller they bought for them is massive, it looks like a tank. It barely fits through a regular doorway. But, uh... yeah, no big stressful events or anything like that. I don't know if they'd call it significant, like medically. But like I keep saying, the only reason I even mentioned it is because my eating has just been so off for the last few weeks. I end up skipping breakfast and lunch without even trying to, and then my wife makes dinner and I just pick at it. And now my jeans are loose. 

She actually tried to make a roast the other night to get me to eat. She got this huge cut of beef from the butcher downtown. Have you been down there recently? And the guy working the booth in the garage was watching something on his phone with the volume all the way up, completely ignoring everyone. It's like, I'm paying fifteen dollars an hour to park in a concrete box, the least you could do is pretend to do your job. 

But yeah, I don't know if a doctor would say it's a significant amount of weight, but it's just from my appetite being so messed up lately.

But anyway, yeah, I must be dropping some weight because my jeans are definitely starting to feel loose.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your weight loss is noticeable to other people, like your wife?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah, like I was just saying, she definitely notices when I just pick at the dinner she cooks. It's hard for her not to see it when my eating has been this off for the past few weeks and I'm skipping breakfast and lunch without even meaning to. She puts a lot of effort into dinner, too. It's supposed to make things crispy without oil, but honestly, it just seems like a tiny, really loud convection oven to me. \n\nI tried making sweet potato fries in it the other day and they just came out completely soggy. Sweet potatoes are weird like that, they never get as crispy as regular potatoes unless you coat them in cornstarch first. I saw that trick on a cooking show once. The host had this crazy handlebar mustache, like one of those old-timey strongmen at the circus. You know, the guys who wear the striped leotards and lift the giant round weights? I always wondered if those weights were actually heavy or if they were just hollow props made of wood or something. It seems like it would be a huge liability to have actual 500-pound iron balls rolling around a circus tent with all those kids everywhere. \n\nBut, um... what was I saying? Oh, right, my wife noticing. Yeah, she probably notices the weight itself too, since, like I mentioned, my jeans are really starting to feel loose on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think someone who hasn't seen you in a while would notice that you've lost weight?\n\nPatient: I mean, probably? They brought this bottle of wine that was supposed to be from some boutique vineyard in California. I don't really know much about wine, honestly. IPAs are everywhere now, and I just don't get the appeal. Why would you want to drink something that tastes bitter on purpose? It's like drinking black coffee, which I also can't do. I need at least a little bit of cream in it. My dad used to drink his coffee black as night, said it put hair on his chest. He worked construction for thirty years, so I guess he needed the caffeine. He used to wake up at 4 AM every single day. I could never do that. \n\nBut yeah, if someone saw me now compared to a few weeks ago, they'd probably notice I've dropped some weight since my eating has been so off.\n\nDoctor: Did you want to lose weight when you started losing it?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I wasn't trying to diet or anything. Like I've been saying, the whole reason I even brought it up is just because my eating has been so off for the past few weeks. I'm skipping breakfast and lunch without even meaning to, and then picking at dinner, and now my jeans are loose. It's actually really annoying because I hate buying new jeans. Finding the right fit is impossible now because everything is \"slim fit\" or \"athletic taper.\" I tried on a pair at the mall last year and I couldn't even get my calves through the legs. Who are they making these pants for? You have to have toothpicks for legs. \n\nThe mall is so depressing nowadays anyway, half the stores are boarded up. The only things left are pretzel stands and those kiosks that sell cheap phone cases. The guys working there are so pushy, too. They literally step in front of you while you're walking to try and clean your screen or sell you a remote-control helicopter. It's like, no thanks, I can wipe my own phone on my shirt, you know? I did buy a pretzel last time I was there, though. Cinnamon sugar. It was okay, but they never give you enough icing to dip it in. \n\nBut, um... yeah. I definitely wasn't trying to lose weight on purpose. It just kind of happened because I haven't been eating right.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're losing weight in a way that's not healthy for you?\n\nPatient: I mean, probably not healthy, yeah. Like I've been saying this whole time, the main reason I even brought the weight up is just because my eating has been so off for the past few weeks. Skipping breakfast and lunch by accident, picking at the dinners my wife makes, my jeans getting baggy... none of that feels like a healthy way to drop pounds. \n\nHealthy is a weird concept nowadays anyway, isn't it? Like, my wife takes all these expensive gummy vitamins now because she saw some influencer on TikTok pushing them. I really don't get TikTok. It's just people doing the exact same hand dances over and over in their kitchens. And their kitchens are always completely spotless! Like, who has bright white marble countertops and actually cooks? If you spill one drop of red wine or tomato sauce on that, it's stained forever. We have this ugly brown laminate in our kitchen. It was there when we moved in. The landlord said he'd update it before we signed the lease, but you know how landlords are. They promise you the moon and then completely ignore your texts when the sink backs up. Which actually happened to us on Thanksgiving two years ago. We had to call an emergency plumber on a holiday, and the guy charged an absolute fortune. He tracked mud all over the hallway carpet, too, and didn't even apologize. \n\nBut, uh... what were we talking about? Oh, right, if the weight loss is healthy. Yeah, I guess probably not, since it's just happening because my appetite is gone and I'm not eating right.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your lack of interest in activities like playing guitar or watching videos is related to your weight loss?\n\nPatient: I don't know if they're related, really. It's more just... I don't know. Most days lately I wake up with this heavy, flat feeling, you know? Like the apartment is just too quiet. And even picking up my guitar doesn't really lift me out of it. \n\nI actually haven't changed the strings on that guitar in probably a year. You're supposed to change them every couple of months if you play a lot, but they're so expensive now. Everything is expensive. I went to buy a pack of strings at the local music shop, and the guy behind the counter tried to upsell me on these coated ones that supposedly last longer. He kept talking about the tone and the resonance and all this technical stuff. I just wanted regular strings. The guy had this huge beard, too, like one of those lumberjack beards. I don't know how guys eat with those things. I tried to grow a beard once, maybe like five years ago? It just came in all patchy and itchy. My wife hated it. She said I looked like a guy who sleeps on a bus bench. Which, honestly, she wasn't wrong. I shaved it off after like three weeks. \n\nBut yeah, I don't know if the weight loss is connected to not wanting to play or whatever. It's just... I wake up feeling really flat and heavy, and nothing really seems worth doing.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your weight loss is something that's happened without you trying to make it happen?\n\nPatient: Yeah, exactly. I feel like a broken record here, but I definitely wasn't trying to make it happen. Like I've been saying, the whole reason I even brought it up is just that for the past few weeks my eating has been completely off. I'll skip breakfast and lunch without even meaning to, and then I just pick at dinner, even when my wife cooked. And now my jeans are starting to feel loose. \n\nShe actually made this huge baked ziti two nights ago, which is usually my favorite. She uses this specific ricotta cheese with a red lid, but she had to go to three different grocery stores just to find it because our local place is always out of stock. Grocery stores are a complete nightmare right now anyway. They keep moving all the items around. Like, why put the bread in aisle four one week and then move it to aisle nine the next? You end up walking in circles for twenty minutes just looking for a loaf of sourdough. And don't even get me started on those self-checkout machines. They replaced all the actual cashiers with these robots that just yell at you about an \"unexpected item in the bagging area.\" It's like, it's a lemon, just let me buy it! I swear the machine is just waiting to freeze up so the one teenager working the whole section has to come over and scan his little barcode badge. \n\nBut, um... yeah. I didn't try to lose the weight. It's just happening because my eating has been so messed up lately.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you look thinner now than you did a few weeks ago?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. I can see it in the mirror, and like I've been saying, my jeans are getting really baggy on me. It's just because my eating has been so completely off for the last few weeks. Skipping breakfast and lunch without even realizing it, and then barely touching the dinners my wife makes. \n\nShe bought me these jeans, actually. From one of those outlet malls out past the city. We drove out there on a Saturday, which was a terrible idea. The traffic was unbelievable. People just camp in the left lane going exactly the speed limit, completely oblivious to the line of cars piling up behind them. I don't understand how people get their licenses and then completely forget the basic rules of the road. And then when we finally got to the mall, the parking lot was like the Hunger Games. We circled for twenty minutes before this guy in a giant pickup truck finally pulled out. Of course, he took up two spaces while he was parked there. I don't know why you need a truck that big unless you're hauling lumber every day. The bed of his truck didn't even have a scratch on it, so you know he just uses it to go to the grocery store. \n\nBut yeah, the jeans she got me that day, they're definitely fitting a lot looser now. So I definitely look thinner than I did a few weeks ago.\n\nDoctor: Do you think a doctor would be concerned about the amount of weight you've lost?\n\nPatient: I mean, I don't know if a doctor would be, like, alarmed by it. But it's definitely noticeable. Like I've been saying, the whole reason I even brought it up is just that for the past few weeks my eating has been totally off. I'll just skip breakfast and lunch without even meaning to, and then I just pick at dinner, even when my wife cooked. And my jeans are starting to feel loose. \n\nSpeaking of doctors, though, I actually had to take my dog to the vet last week because he ate a sock. A whole tube sock. I don't even know how he swallowed it. He's a golden retriever, so he's basically a vacuum cleaner with fur. The vet clinic was freezing, too. They always keep those places at like meat-locker temperatures. And the receptionist had this weird little fan blowing right on her face, even though it was already freezing. She was clicking her pen the entire time I was filling out the paperwork. Click, click, click. It was driving me insane. I almost asked her to stop, but you don't want to make the people who are about to operate on your dog mad at you, you know? They ended up not having to do surgery, thankfully. They just gave him some medicine to make him throw it up. It cost me four hundred dollars just to watch my dog puke up a gym sock. \n\nBut, um... yeah, I don't know if a doctor would be super concerned about the weight loss, but it's just happening because my eating has been so off lately.\n\nDoctor: Do you think a doctor would consider the weight you've lost to be a significant amount?\n\nPatient: I don't know if they'd call it significant, like medically. I end up skipping breakfast and lunch without even trying to, and then my wife makes dinner and I just pick at it. And now my jeans are loose. \n\nShe actually tried to make a roast the other night to get me to eat. She got this huge cut of beef from the butcher downtown. Have you been down there recently? They completely tore up Main Street for that new water main project, and it's a total disaster. There's orange cones everywhere, and they blocked off all the street parking. So you have to park in this sketchy garage three blocks away where the elevator always smells like stale beer. And the guy working the booth in the garage was watching something on his phone with the volume all the way up, completely ignoring everyone. It's like, I'm paying fifteen dollars an hour to park in a concrete box, the least you could do is pretend to do your job. \n\nBut yeah, I don't know if a doctor would say it's a significant amount of weight, but it's just from my appetite being so messed up lately."
